  240|     if adm.get('gate_id'):
  241|         w = c.get('trusted_fixture_inputs', {}).get('gate_verifications', {}).get(adm['gate_id'])
  242|         out['verification_source'] = 'test_mock' if isinstance(w, dict) and w.get('source') == 'test_mock' else 'unverified'
  243|         if isinstance(w, dict) and w.get('payload_sha256'): out['verification_payload_sha256'] = w['payload_sha256']
  244|     return out
  245| 
  246| 
  247| # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ corpus checks
  248| def check_case(c, rates, F):
  249|     cid = c.get('case_id', '?')
  250|     for k in ('schema', 'contract', 'case_id', 'group', 'role', 'class', 'reachability', 'findings', 'provenance', 'invariants',
  251|               'controls', 'inputs', 'evaluations', 'expected', 'ambiguity', 'notes', 'historical', 'rationale', 'revisions', 'revisions_r3',
  252|               'revisions_r4', 'revisions_r5', 'revisions_r6', 'revisions_r7', 'profile'):
  253|         if k not in c: F.add(cid, f'missing field {k}')
  254|     if c.get('schema') != SCHEMA or c.get('contract') != CONTRACT: F.add(cid, 'schema or contract version mismatch')
  255|     if c.get('class') not in CLASSES: F.add(cid, f'bad class {c.get("class")}')
  256|     if c.get('reachability') not in REACH: F.add(cid, 'bad reachability')
  257|     if c.get('role') not in ROLES: F.add(cid, 'bad role')
  258|     if not c.get('provenance', {}).get('origin'): F.add(cid, 'provenance.origin missing')
  259|     for inv in c.get('invariants', []):
  260|         if inv not in INVARIANTS: F.add(cid, f'unknown invariant {inv}')
  261|     if c.get('role') == 'finding' and not c.get('controls'): F.add(cid, 'finding without a control')
  262|     prof = c.get('profile')
  263|     if prof not in PROFILES: F.add(cid, f'bad profile {prof}')
  264|     if prof == 'current_producer':            # OQ-1: no hypothetical gate or authority evidence on current-producer cases
  265|         if c['inputs'].get('gates') or any(o.get('admission_authority') or o.get('closure_record') or any(a.get('gate_id') for a in o['admissions'])
  266|                                             for e in c['evaluations'] for o in e['objectives']):
  267|             F.add(cid, 'current_producer case carries future gate or authority evidence (OQ-1)')
  268|         if c.get('class') == 'future_vertical': F.add(cid, 'current_producer case classed future_vertical')
  269|     if prof == 'future_qualified_gate':
  270|         if c.get('reachability') != 'producer_unreachable' or c.get('class') != 'future_vertical' or c.get('historical') != {}:
  271|             F.add(cid, 'future_qualified_gate case must be producer_unreachable, class future_vertical, with no historical comparison')
  272|     for r in c.get('revisions', []) + c.get('revisions_r3', []) + c.get('revisions_r4', []) + c.get('revisions_r5', []) + c.get('revisions_r6', []) + c.get('revisions_r7', []):
  273|         if set(r) != {'rule', 'field', 'from', 'to'} or not r['rule']: F.add(cid, f'malformed revision entry {r}')
  274|     if c.get('added_in') not in (None,) + tuple(sorted(ADDED_IN)): F.add(cid, 'bad added_in')
  275|     if c.get('added_in') == '1.2-s1' and (c.get('revisions') or c.get('revisions_r3')): F.add(cid, 'a case added in revision 3 has no earlier expectation to revise')
  276|     if c.get('added_in') == '1.3-s1' and (c.get('revisions') or c.get('revisions_r3') or c.get('revisions_r4')): F.add(cid, 'a case added in revision 4 has no earlier expectation to revise')
  277|     if c.get('added_in') == '1.4-s1' and any(c.get(k) for k in ('revisions', 'revisions_r3', 'revisions_r4', 'revisions_r5')): F.add(cid, 'a case added in revision 5 has no earlier expectation to revise')
  278|     inp = c['inputs']
  279|     for name, (digest, _) in rates.items():
  280|         if inp['rates'].get(name) != digest: F.add(cid, f'rate digest mismatch for {name}')
  281|     sessions = {s['alias']: s for s in inp['sessions']}
  282|     for s in inp['sessions']:
  283|         for turn, log in s['logs'].items():
  284|             if not isinstance(log.get('lines'), list) or not isinstance(log.get('final_newline'), bool): F.add(cid, f'bad log {s["alias"]}/{turn}')
  285|     lines, order = source_lines(c)
  286|     ex = c['expected']
  287|     covered, keys = [], set()
  288|     for a in ex['attempts']:
  289|         check_attempt(cid, c, a, lines, rates, F)
  290|         keys.add(a['key']); covered += a['evidence']
  291|     for x in ex['non_attempt'] + ex['unreadable']:
  292|         covered.append(x['evidence'])
  293|         for b in x.get('blocking', []):
  294|             if b not in BLOCKING: F.add(cid, f'unknown blocking {b}')
  295|     for r in covered:
  296|         if r not in lines: F.add(cid, f'evidence ref {r} not in inputs')
  297|     dup = {r for r in covered if covered.count(r) > 1}
  298|     if dup: F.add(cid, f'evidence counted twice (INV-F6): {sorted(dup)}')
  299|     missing = set(lines) - set(covered)
  300|     if missing: F.add(cid, f'evidence not accounted for (INV-F6): {sorted(missing)}')
  301|     for alias in sessions:
  302|         if alias not in ex['lifecycle']: F.add(cid, f'lifecycle missing for {alias}')
  303|     for alias, l in ex['lifecycle'].items():
  304|         if l.get('state') not in LIFE or l.get('clock') not in ('trusted', 'untrusted') or not isinstance(l.get('reopened'), bool):
  305|             F.add(cid, f'bad lifecycle {alias}'); continue
  306|         s = sessions.get(alias)
  307|         if s is None: continue                           # S08 pair: journal-only cases (revision-1 oddity)
  308|         if l['reopened'] != reopened_of(s): F.add(cid, f'lifecycle {alias}: reopened {l["reopened"]} contradicts its rows (L-5, R-10)')
  309|         last = s['lifecycle'][-1] if s['lifecycle'] else None
  310|         if l['state'] == 'retired' and not (last and last['event'] == 'retire' and last.get('at')):
  311|             F.add(cid, f'lifecycle {alias}: retired but the last row is not a dated retire (L-4)')
  312|         if l['state'] == 'open' and last and last['event'] == 'retire' and last.get('at') and l['clock'] == 'trusted':
  313|             F.add(cid, f'lifecycle {alias}: open but the last row is a dated retire (L-4)')
  314|     names = [e['name'] for e in c['evaluations']]
  315|     if len(names) != len(set(names)): F.add(cid, 'duplicate evaluation names')
  316|     for e in c['evaluations']:
  317|         check_evaluation(cid, c, e, keys, sessions, lines, order, F)
  318| 
  319| 
  320| def check_attempt(cid, c, a, lines, rates, F):
  321|     k = a['key']; m = KEY.match(k)
  322|     if not m: F.add(cid, f'bad key {k}'); return
  323|     kind, alias, turn, field, num = m.groups()
  324|     src = f'{alias}/turn-{turn}'
  325|     rows = [parse(lines.get(r)) for r in a['evidence']]
  326|     if kind == 'row' and f'{src}:{num}' not in a['evidence']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: row key line not in its evidence')
  327|     if kind in ('res', 'adm') and not any(r and r.get('event') in ('usd_reserved', 'subscription_proxy_admitted') and str(r.get('rid')) == num for r in rows):
  328|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: no reservation/admission with that rid in its evidence')
  329|     corr = a['correlation']                                                       # R-06: key join plus auxiliary rows
  330|     if corr.get('kind') not in KEY_JOIN: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad key join {corr.get("kind")}')
  331|     for ax in corr.get('auxiliary', []):
  332|         row = parse(lines.get(ax.get('evidence')))
  333|         if ax.get('evidence') not in a['evidence'] or ax.get('rule') not in SHAPE_RULES or row is None: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad auxiliary {ax}'); continue
  334|         want = {'RS-1': row.get('event') == 'stream_error' and 'detail' in row, 'RS-2': row.get('event') == 'stream_error' and 'detail' not in row,
  335|                 'RS-3': row.get('event') == 'response_model_refused'}[ax['rule']]
  336|         if not want: F.add(cid, f'{k}: auxiliary {ax["rule"]} does not match its row shape (E-3)')
  337|     s = a['settlement']
  338|     if s.get('decision') not in DECISIONS: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad decision'); return
  339|     if s['decision'] == 'valid' and s.get('basis') not in BASES: F.add(cid, f'{k}: valid without a basis')
  340|     if s['decision'] != 'rejected' and s.get('reasons'): F.add(cid, f'{k}: reasons on a non-rejected decision')
  341|     if s['decision'] == 'rejected' and not s.get('reasons'): F.add(cid, f'{k}: rejected without reasons')
  342|     for r in s.get('reasons', []):
  343|         if r not in REJECT: F.add(cid, f'{k}: unknown reject reason {r}')
  344|     ob = a['obligation']; obt = (ob.get('state'), ob.get('amount_source'))
  345|     if obt not in OBLIGATION: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad obligation {obt}')
  346|     rp_want = replacement_of(a)
  347|     if a.get('replacement') != rp_want: F.add(cid, f'{k}: replacement {a.get("replacement")} != {rp_want} (2.1, A-01, INV-F13)')
  348|     want = {'valid': {'usage': ('closed', 'settlement_amount' if rp_want['eligible'] else 'reservation_bound'), 'reservation': ('closed', 'reservation_confirmed'),
  349|                       'upstream_error': ('closed', 'reservation_confirmed'), 'error_status': ('closed', 'reservation_bound')}.get(s.get('basis')),
  350|             'absent': ('outstanding', 'reservation_bound'), 'rejected': ('outstanding', 'reservation_bound'),
  351|             'conflicting': ('outstanding', 'reservation_bound'), 'not_applicable': ('none', 'none')}[s['decision']]
  352|     if kind == 'row' and s['decision'] in ('rejected', 'conflicting'): want = ('none', 'none')
  353|     if ob.get('state') == 'closed' and ob.get('by') not in [ref for ref, row in zip(a['evidence'], rows) if row and isinstance(row.get('usd'), dict)]: F.add(cid, f'{k}: closed obligation lacks settlement evidence by reference')
  354|     if obt != want: F.add(cid, f'{k}: obligation {obt} does not match decision (want {want}) (INV-F1)')
  355|     if ob.get('state') == 'closed' and not (s['decision'] == 'valid' and corr.get('kind') == 'exact'):
  356|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: closed without a Valid settlement on an Exact key join (INV-F1, R-06)')
  357|     for b in a['blocking']:
  358|         if b not in BLOCKING and not re.fullmatch(r'MalformedEvidence\([A-Za-z_]+\)', b): F.add(cid, f'{k}: unknown blocking {b}')
  359|     for d in a['diagnostics']:
  360|         if d not in DIAGNOSTICS: F.add(cid, f'{k}: unknown diagnostic {d}')
  361|     if s['decision'] == 'rejected' and 'SettlementRejected' not in a['blocking']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: rejected without SettlementRejected')
  362|     if s['decision'] == 'conflicting' and 'SettlementConflict' not in a['blocking']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: conflicting without SettlementConflict')
  363|     fw = a.get('forwarding')                                                       # R-13
  364|     if fw not in FORWARDING: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad or missing forwarding fact')
  365|     elif fw != ('forwarded' if any(forwarded_row(lines.get(r)) for r in a['evidence']) else 'unknown'):
  366|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: forwarding {fw} contradicts its evidence rows (6.1, R-13)')
  367|     ex_ = a.get('execution')                                                       # R-07
  368|     if ex_ not in EXECUTION: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad or missing execution fact')
  369|     elif ex_ != ('terminal' if any(r and r.get('event') in TERMINAL_EVENTS for r in rows) else 'not_terminal'):
  370|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: execution {ex_} contradicts its evidence rows (6.2)')
  371|     raw_settlement_checks(cid, c, a, rows, lines, rates, F)
  372|     v = a['valuation']; st = v.get('state')
  373|     if st == 'valued':
  374|         lows, highs = [], []
  375|         for d in v['derivations']:
  376|             try: p = price(d, rates)
  377|             except (KeyError, ValueError) as e: F.add(cid, f'{k}: derivation not computable: {e}'); continue
  378|             if p != nano(d['nano']): F.add(cid, f'{k}: derivation {d["model"]} states {d["nano"]}, rate files give {p}')
  379|             if d['provider'] + '/' + d['model'] not in v['identities']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: derivation model {d["model"]} not among identities')
  380|             if d['for'] in ('low', 'both'): lows.append(p)
  381|             if d['for'] in ('high', 'both'): highs.append(p)
  382|             if d['provider'] == 'anthropic' and d.get('split') != 'ignored':
  383|                 u = d['usage']
  384|                 rem = (u.get('cache_creation_input_tokens') or 0) - (u.get('ephemeral_5m_input_tokens') or 0) - (u.get('ephemeral_1h_input_tokens') or 0)
  385|                 if rem > 0 and d['for'] in ('low', 'high') and d.get('remainder_rate') != {'low': 'min', 'high': 'max'}[d['for']]:
  386|                     F.add(cid, f'{k}: residual Anthropic writes must be at min for the low and max for the high (V-4, R-15)')
  387|         if lows and min(lows) != nano(v['low']): F.add(cid, f'{k}: valuation low {v["low"]} != derivations {min(lows)}')
  388|         if highs and max(highs) != nano(v['high']): F.add(cid, f'{k}: valuation high {v["high"]} != derivations {max(highs)}')
  389|         if nano(v['low']) > nano(v['high']): F.add(cid, f'{k}: valuation low > high')
  390|         if len(v['identities']) > 1 and 'IdentityContradiction' not in a['blocking']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: several identities without IdentityContradiction (D6)')
  391|     tiers = set(rates['openai_api_rates.json'][1]['tier_multiplier'])             # R-03, from raw rows
  392|     oa_req = [r for r in rows if r and r.get('event') == 'request' and r.get('provider', 'openai') != 'anthropic' and isinstance(r.get('usage'), dict)
  393|               and any(x is not None for x in r['usage'].values())]
  394|     if st == 'valued' and any(d['provider'] == 'openai' for d in v['derivations']) and any(
  395|             not (isinstance(r.get('observed_tier'), str) and r['observed_tier'] in tiers) for r in oa_req):
  396|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: OpenAI tier missing or unrecognised but valued as a finite range (V-3, R-03)')
  397|     x = a['exposure']
  398|     bl = a.get('billing')
  399|     if x['pool'] == 'codex':
  400|         if set(x) != {'pool', 'billing'}: F.add(cid, f'{k}: codex pool carries money (INV-F9)')
  401|         if bl != 'not_applicable': F.add(cid, f'{k}: codex attempt billing must be not_applicable')
  402|         return
  403|     lo, kd, hi = att_exp(a)
  404|     if x['low']['basis'] not in LOW_BASES or x['high']['basis'] not in HIGH_BASES: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad basis')
  405|     if kd not in ('exact', 'at_least'): F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad high kind')
  406|     if lo > hi: F.add(cid, f'{k}: low > high (INV-F11)')
  407|     if kd == 'at_least' and 'UnresolvedExposure' not in a['blocking']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: AtLeast without UnresolvedExposure (D5)')
  408|     if lo > 0 and x['low']['basis'] != 'usage_valuation': F.add(cid, f'{k}: non-zero low not supported by usage valuation (INV-F11)')
  409|     if bl not in BILLING or bl != ('established' if kd == 'exact' else 'unresolved'):
  410|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: billing {bl} does not follow the high kind {kd} (INV-F12, R-07)')
  411|     table_x(cid, k, a, lo, kd, hi, F, lines)
  412| 
  413| 
  414| def usage_bound(a):
  415|     """FB-1: a complete usage valuation exists (as the expected valuation states it)."""
  416|     s, v = a['settlement'], a['valuation']
  417|     if v.get('state') != 'valued' or not v.get('complete'): return None
  418|     if s['decision'] == 'valid' and s.get('basis') == 'usage': return nano(v['high'])
  419|     if s['decision'] == 'not_applicable': return nano(v['high'])
  420|     return None
  421| 
  422| 
  423| def replacement_of(a):
  424|     """2.1 (1.4-s1): Eligible iff Valid(Usage) with a complete usage valuation over established sets."""
  425|     s_, v, x = a['settlement'], a['valuation'], a['exposure']
  426|     if x.get('pool') == 'codex' or a['obligation'].get('state') == 'none': return {'eligible': False, 'reason': 'NoReservation'}
  427|     if not (s_.get('decision') == 'valid' and s_.get('basis') == 'usage'): return {'eligible': False, 'reason': 'NotUsageSettlement'}
  428|     if v.get('state') == 'valued' and v.get('complete'): return {'eligible': True, 'reason': 'UsageBound'}
  429|     return {'eligible': False, 'reason': 'UsageIncomplete' if v.get('state') == 'valued' else 'UsageUnpriceable'}
  430| 
  431| 
  432| def high_of(terms):
  433|     terms = [t for t in terms if t[0] is not None]
  434|     best = max(t[0] for t in terms)
  435|     return best, sorted((t for t in terms if t[0] == best), key=lambda t: TIE[t[1]])[0][1]
  436| 
  437| 
  438| def raw_reservation(a, lines):
  439|     """X-0 (1.4-s1, A-09): the largest distinct well-formed reservation amount in the attempt's own rows."""
  440|     vals = set(); n = 0; seen = set()
  441|     for r in a['evidence']:
  442|         row = parse(lines.get(r))
  443|         if row and row.get('event') == 'usd_reserved':
  444|             key = json.dumps(row, sort_keys=True)
  445|             if key in seen: continue
  446|             seen.add(key); n += 1
  447|             amt = row.get('reserved_usd')
  448|             if isinstance(amt, (int, float)) and not isinstance(amt, bool): vals.add(int(Decimal(str(amt)) * 10 ** 9))
  449|     return (max(vals) if vals else None), n
  450| 
  451| 
  452| def table_x(cid, k, a, lo, kd, hi, F, lines=None):
  453|     """Consistency of expected exposure with TRANSITIONS Table X (1.4-s1), including the X-0 basis on ties."""
  454|     s, v = a['settlement'], a['valuation']
  455|     R = nano(s['reserved']) if 'reserved' in s else None    # settlement.reserved is the settlement's claim; R is the reservation rows' amount
  456|     if lines is not None:
  457|         Rraw, nres = raw_reservation(a, lines)
  458|         if Rraw is not None: R = Rraw                                               # X-0 (1.4-s1): the largest distinct reservation (A-09)
  459|     ch = nano(s['charged']) if 'charged' in s else None
  460|     vl, vh = (nano(v['low']), nano(v['high'])) if v.get('state') == 'valued' else (None, None)
  461|     ph = nano(v['priced_high']) if v.get('state') == 'unpriceable' and v.get('priced_high') is not None else None
  462|     sl = nano(v['supported_low']) if v.get('state') == 'unpriceable' and v.get('supported_low') is not None else 0
  463|     known_v = vh if vh is not None else ph
  464|     exp = None; terms = None
  465|     d, b = s['decision'], s.get('basis')
  466|     if d == 'valid' and b == 'usage' and ch is not None:
  467|         if v.get('state') == 'valued' and v.get('complete'): terms = [(ch, 'proxy_charge'), (vh, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (vl, 'exact')     # FB-1, eligible
  468|         elif R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation'), (ch, 'proxy_charge'), (known_v, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (vl if vl is not None else sl, 'at_least')  # A-01
  469|     elif d == 'valid' and b == 'reservation' and R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation'), (known_v, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (vl if vl is not None else sl, 'at_least')
  470|     elif d == 'valid' and b == 'upstream_error' and R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation')]; exp = (0, 'at_least')
  471|     elif d == 'valid' and b == 'error_status' and R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation'), (known_v, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (vl if vl is not None else sl, 'at_least')   # A-02
  472|     elif d == 'absent' and R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation')]; exp = (0, 'at_least')
  473|     elif d in ('rejected', 'conflicting') and R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation'), (ch, 'proxy_charge'), (known_v, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (0, 'at_least')  # R-05
  474|     elif d == 'not_applicable' and v.get('state') == 'valued' and v.get('complete'): terms = [(vh, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (vl, 'exact')
  475|     if exp is not None:
  476|         h_amt, h_basis = high_of(terms)
  477|         if (exp[0], exp[1], h_amt) != (lo, kd, hi): F.add(cid, f'{k}: exposure {(lo, kd, hi)} != Table X {(exp[0], exp[1], h_amt)}')
  478|         elif a['exposure']['high'].get('basis') != h_basis: F.add(cid, f'{k}: high basis {a["exposure"]["high"].get("basis")} != X-0 tie order {h_basis}')
  479|     ub = usage_bound(a)
  480|     if kd == 'exact' and ub is None: F.add(cid, f'{k}: Exact high without a usage bound (FB-1, FB-2)')
  481|     if kd == 'exact' and a['exposure']['high']['basis'] == 'proxy_charge' and not (ub is not None and ub <= hi):
  482|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: Exact high with basis proxy_charge needs a usage bound below it (FB-1, R-02)')
  483| 
  484| 
  485| def openai_range(u, model, mult, rates):
  486|     """V-4 (1.4-s1, A-04) from raw counts: (low, high) over the vertices of unknown cache counts, or 'malformed'."""
  487|     oa = rates['openai_api_rates.json'][1]; r = oa['models'][model]
  488|     i, o = u.get('input_tokens'), u.get('output_tokens')
  489|     if not all(isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool) and x >= 0 for x in (i, o)): return 'malformed'
  490|     def state(key):
  491|         if key not in u or u[key] is None: return None
  492|         x = u[key]
  493|         return x if isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool) and x >= 0 else 'malformed'
  494|     c, w = state('cached_tokens'), state('cache_write_tokens')
  495|     if 'malformed' in (c, w): return 'malformed'
  496|     if c is not None and w is not None and c + w > i: return 'malformed'
  497|     if c is not None and w is not None: pts = [(c, w)]
  498|     elif c is not None: pts = [(c, 0), (c, i - c)]
  499|     elif w is not None: pts = [(0, w), (i - w, w)]
  500|     else: pts = [(0, 0), (i, 0), (0, i)]
  501|     long_ = i > int(oa['long_input_threshold'])
  502|     mi = Fraction(oa['long_input_multiplier']) if long_ else 1; mo = Fraction(oa['long_output_multiplier']) if long_ else 1
  503|     m = Fraction(Decimal(mult))
  504|     prices = [(i - cc - ww) * per_token(r['input'], m * mi) + cc * per_token(r['cached_input'], m * mi) + ww * per_token(r['cache_write'], m * mi)
  505|               + o * per_token(r['output'], m * mo) for cc, ww in pts]
  506|     return min(prices), max(prices)
  507| 
  508| 
  509| def raw_usage_policy_checks(cid, a, rows, F):
  510|     """Root P1/P2 assertions; auxiliary counts do not establish a billing report."""
  511|     requests = [r for r in rows if r and r.get('event') == 'request']
  512|     v = a['valuation']; k = a['key']
  513|     for row in requests:
  514|         fam = PROVIDERS.get(row.get('provider', 'openai'))
  515|         billing = ('input_tokens', 'output_tokens', 'cached_tokens', 'cache_write_tokens') if fam == 'openai' else ('input_tokens', 'output_tokens', 'cache_read_input_tokens', 'cache_creation_input_tokens', 'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens', 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens')
  516|         u = row.get('usage')
  517|         if not isinstance(u, dict):
  518|             if u is not None and v.get('state') == 'valued': F.add(cid, f'{k}: non-object usage valued (UsageShape)')
  519|             continue
  520|         report = any(u.get(f) is not None for f in billing)
  521|         if a['settlement']['decision'] not in ('rejected', 'conflicting'):
  522|             for f in billing:
  523|                 if u.get(f) is not None and not (type(u[f]) is int and u[f] >= 0) and 'MalformedEvidence(' + f + ')' not in a['blocking']:
  524|                     F.add(cid, f'{k}: malformed billing evidence lacks field diagnostic (P2)')
  525| 
  526|         contrary = ('cache_read_input_tokens', 'cache_creation_input_tokens', 'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens', 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens') if fam == 'openai' else ('cached_tokens', 'cache_write_tokens')
  527|         mismatch = fam is None or any(u.get(f) is not None for f in contrary)
  528|         if mismatch and v.get('state') == 'valued': F.add(cid, f'{k}: incompatible provider billing schema priced (UsageSchemaMismatch)')
  529|         if not report and len(requests) == 1 and v.get('state') == 'valued': F.add(cid, f'{k}: auxiliary-only usage cannot establish priceable report (P2)')
  530|         if fam == 'anthropic' and v.get('state') == 'valued':
  531|             tier = row.get('observed_tier')
  532|             if tier is not None and tier != 'standard': F.add(cid, f'{k}: unestablished Anthropic tier priced (P2)')
  533|         if fam == 'anthropic' and v.get('complete'):
  534|             total = u.get('cache_creation_input_tokens')
  535|             parts = [u.get(f) if u.get(f) is not None else 0 for f in ('ephemeral_5m_input_tokens', 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens')]
  536|             if type(total) is not int or any(type(x) is not int or x < 0 for x in parts) or sum(parts) > total:
  537|                 F.add(cid, f'{k}: inconsistent lifetime split cannot be complete (P2)')
  538|         if v.get('complete'):
  539|             status = row.get('http_status'); success = row.get('terminal') in ({'response.completed', 'response.done'} if fam == 'openai' else {'message_stop'})
  540|             required = ('input_tokens', 'output_tokens') if fam == 'openai' else ('input_tokens', 'output_tokens', 'cache_read_input_tokens', 'cache_creation_input_tokens')
  541|             counts = all(isinstance(u.get(f), int) and not isinstance(u.get(f), bool) and u[f] >= 0 for f in required)
  542|             if not (type(status) is int and status == 200 and success and counts and not mismatch): F.add(cid, f'{k}: complete valuation lacks status+terminal+counts conjunction (P2)')
  543|         for f in ('reasoning_tokens',) if fam == 'openai' else ():
  544|             if u.get(f) is not None and not (type(u[f]) is int and u[f] >= 0):
  545|                 if 'MalformedEvidence(' + f + ')' not in a['blocking']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: malformed auxiliary evidence lacks field diagnostic (P2)')
  546| 
  547| 
  548| def raw_settlement_checks(cid, c, a, rows, lines, rates, F):
  549|     """Settlement conditions and valuation re-derived from raw producer rows where the contract makes them mechanical:
  550|     status by path (5.7, A-06), model canonicalisation (MC-1..MC-3, A-05), and the OpenAI valuation range (V-4, A-04)."""
  551|     raw_usage_policy_checks(cid, a, rows, F)
  552|     k = a['key']; s_ = a['settlement']; d, b = s_.get('decision'), s_.get('basis')
  553|     setl = [r for r in rows if r and isinstance(r.get('usd'), dict) and r.get('event') in ('request', 'upstream_error')]
  554|     distinct_reservations = {lines[ref] for ref in a['evidence'] if isinstance(parse(lines.get(ref)), dict) and parse(lines[ref]).get('event') == 'usd_reserved'}
  555|     if len(distinct_reservations) != 1 and setl:
  556|         forbidden = set(s_.get('reasons', [])) & {'ReservedMismatch', 'Order', 'KeepAll'}
  557|         if forbidden: F.add(cid, f'{k}: reservation-relative checks inapplicable without UniqueReservation: {sorted(forbidden)} (P3)')
  558|         if d == 'valid': F.add(cid, f'{k}: valid settlement without UniqueReservation (P3)')
  559| 
  560|     if d == 'valid' and setl:
  561|         row = setl[0]; st = row.get('http_status', '__absent__')
  562|         isint = isinstance(st, int) and not isinstance(st, bool)
  563|         ok = {'usage': isint and st == 200, 'error_status': isint and st != 200, 'reservation': (isint if row.get('event') == 'request' else st == '__absent__'),
  564|               'upstream_error': row.get('event') == 'upstream_error' and st == '__absent__'}.get(b, True)
  565|         if not ok: F.add(cid, f'{k}: Valid({b}) but the settlement row status {st!r} is not accepted on this path (5.7, A-06)')
  566|         if b == 'usage':
  567|             alias = k.split(':')[1].split('/')[0]
  568|             launch = next((s2.get('launch') for s2 in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s2['alias'] == alias), None) or {}
  569|             lm = launch.get('model') if isinstance(launch.get('model'), str) else ''
  570|             prov, _, mid = lm.partition('/')
  571|             if prov not in PROVIDERS or not mid or row.get('model') != mid or PROVIDERS.get(row.get('provider', 'openai')) != PROVIDERS.get(prov):
  572|                 F.add(cid, f'{k}: Valid(Usage) but the request model {row.get("model")!r} does not equal the canonical pinned id of {lm!r} (MC-1..MC-3, A-05)')
  573|     # OpenAI valuation range from raw counts (single priced identity, recognised tier)
  574|     tiers = rates['openai_api_rates.json'][1]['tier_multiplier']; models = rates['openai_api_rates.json'][1]['models']
  575|     v = a['valuation']
  576|     for row in rows:
  577|         if not (row and row.get('event') == 'request' and row.get('provider', 'openai') != 'anthropic' and isinstance(row.get('usage'), dict)): continue
  578|         u = row['usage']
  579|         if not any(x is not None for x in u.values()): continue
  580|         tier = row.get('observed_tier')
  581|         ids = {x for x in (row.get('model'), row.get('response_model')) if x}
  582|         if len(ids) != 1 or not (isinstance(tier, str) and tier in tiers): continue
  583|         model = next(iter(ids))
  584|         if model not in models: continue
  585|         rng = openai_range(u, model, str(tiers[tier]), rates)
  586|         if rng != 'malformed' and v.get('state') == 'not_reported':
  587|             F.add(cid, f'{k}: priceable usage in its rows but valuation not_reported (V is independent of the decision, A-06/S06)')
  588|         if rng == 'malformed':
  589|             if v.get('state') == 'valued': F.add(cid, f'{k}: malformed cache counts but valued (V-4, A-04)')
  590|         elif v.get('state') == 'valued' and len(v.get('identities', [])) == 1 and len([x for x in rows if x and x.get('event') == 'request']) == 1:
  591|             if (nano(v['low']), nano(v['high'])) != rng: F.add(cid, f'{k}: valuation {(v["low"], v["high"])} != raw V-4 range {rng} (A-04)')
  592| 
  593| 
  594| def obligation_interval(c, a, lines, order, lifecycle):
  595|     """Contract 11.2 interval with P-2 neighbour bounds: (start_lo, end_hi) as stamps, None meaning unbounded; or
  596|     'unplaceable'. Closed: [reservation, settlement]; Outstanding: [reservation, following dated retirement or open];
  597|     None (unbudgeted, orphan): [first row, last row]."""
  598|     alias = a['key'].split(':')[1].split('/')[0]
  599|     if lifecycle.get(alias, {}).get('clock') == 'untrusted': return 'unplaceable'
  600|     refs = sorted(a['evidence'], key=lambda r: (r.rsplit(':', 1)[0], int(r.rsplit(':', 1)[1])))
  601|     def dated(ref):
  602|         row = parse(lines.get(ref)); at = row.get('at') if row else None
  603|         return at if isinstance(at, str) and STAMP.match(at) else None
  604|     def bounds(ref):
  605|         at = dated(ref)
  606|         if at: return at, at
  607|         src = ref.rsplit(':', 1)[0]; seq = order[src]; i = seq.index(ref)
  608|         before = next((dated(r) for r in reversed(seq[:i]) if dated(r)), None)
  609|         after = next((dated(r) for r in seq[i + 1:] if dated(r)), None)
  610|         if before and after and before > after: return 'inverted', 'inverted'
  611|         return before, after
  612|     first_lo, _ = bounds(refs[0])
  613|     _, last_hi = bounds(refs[-1])
  614|     if 'inverted' in (first_lo, last_hi): return 'unplaceable'
  615|     if a['obligation']['state'] == 'outstanding':
  616|         sess = next(s for s in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s['alias'] == alias)
  617|         rets = [r['at'] for r in sess['lifecycle'] if r['event'] == 'retire' and isinstance(r.get('at'), str) and STAMP.match(r['at'])
  618|                 and (first_lo is None or r['at'] >= first_lo)]
  619|         return first_lo, (min(rets) if rets else None)          # open when no following dated retirement
  620|     return first_lo, last_hi
  621| 
  622| 
  623| def o7_candidates(c, e, a, lines, order, lifecycle, ref):
  624|     """O-7 (1.2-s1): tree members whose window could contain the obligation interval; unplaceable keeps them all."""
  625|     alias = a['key'].split(':')[1].split('/')[0]
  626|     sid = next(s_['session_id'] for s_ in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s_['alias'] == alias)
  627|     iv = obligation_interval(c, a, lines, order, lifecycle); out = []
  628|     for o in e['objectives']:
  629|         if sid not in o['tree_sessions']: continue
  630|         if iv != 'unplaceable':
  631|             lo, hi = iv
  632|             closed = o['closed_at'] if closure_state(o, ref, c.get('profile')) == 'closed' else None
  633|             if (hi is not None and hi < o['started_at']) or (closed and lo is not None and lo > closed): continue
  634|         out.append(o['objective'])
  635|     return sorted(out)
  636| 
  637| 
  638| def admitting_objectives(c, e, a):
  639|     alias = a['key'].split(':')[1].split('/')[0]
  640|     sid = next(s_['session_id'] for s_ in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s_['alias'] == alias)
  641|     return sorted({o['objective'] for o in e['objectives'] for ad in o['admissions'] if ad['session'] == sid})
  642| 
  643| 
  644| def check_evaluation(cid, c, e, keys, sessions, lines, order, F):
  645|     ex, ee = c['expected'], e['expected']
  646|     ref = c['inputs']['reference_time']
  647|     objs = {o['objective']: o for o in e['objectives']}
  648|     attempts = {a['key']: a for a in ex['attempts']}
  649|     for at in ee['attribution']:
  650|         if at['attempt'] not in keys: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: attribution names unknown attempt {at["attempt"]}'); continue
  651|         if at['ownership'] not in OWNERSHIP or at['placement'] not in PLACEMENTS: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: bad attribution {at}')
  652|         if at['ownership'] == 'owned' and at['objective'] not in objs: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: owner {at["objective"]} not an objective')
  653|         a = attempts[at['attempt']]
  654|         if at['ownership'] == 'uncertain':
  655|             if not at.get('candidates'): F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: uncertain without candidates (INV-T2)')
  656|             miss = set(admitting_objectives(c, e, a)) - set(at.get('candidates', []))
  657|             if miss: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: uncertain attempt drops admitting objectives {sorted(miss)} (O-3a, R-08)')
  658|         if at['ownership'] == 'unadmitted':
  659|             if at.get('objective') is not None: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: unadmitted attempt {at["attempt"]} names an owner (O-6)')
  660|             if admitting_objectives(c, e, a): F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: unadmitted although an admission names its session (O-6)')
  661|             want = o7_candidates(c, e, a, lines, order, ex['lifecycle'], ref)
  662|             if sorted(at.get('candidates', [])) != want: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: unadmitted candidates {at.get("candidates")} != O-7 {want} (R-12)')
  663|     attributed = [a['attempt'] for a in ee['attribution']]
  664|     for k in keys:
  665|         if attributed.count(k) != 1: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: attempt {k} attributed {attributed.count(k)} times')
  666|     exp_objs = {o['objective']: o for o in ee['objectives']}
  667|     for name in objs:
  668|         if name not in exp_objs: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: no expected result for objective {name}')
  669|     spent = {}
  670|     for at in ee['attribution']:
  671|         if at['ownership'] == 'owned':
  672|             x = att_exp(attempts[at['attempt']])
  673|             if x: spent.setdefault(at['objective'], {}).setdefault(at['attempt'].split(':')[1].split('/')[0], []).append(x)
  674|     for name, oe in exp_objs.items():
  675|         o = objs.get(name)
  676|         if o is None: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: expected result for unknown objective {name}'); continue
  677|         if oe['status'] not in ('established', 'not_established'): F.add(cid, 'bad status')
  678|         for r in oe['reasons']:
  679|             if r not in OBJ_REASONS and not re.fullmatch(r'MalformedEvidence\([A-Za-z_]+\)', r): F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: unknown reason {r}')
  680|         if (oe['status'] == 'established') != (not oe['reasons']): F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: status/reasons disagree')
  681|         owned = [at for at in ee['attribution'] if at['ownership'] == 'owned' and at['objective'] == name]
  682|         want_provisional = financial_provisional(attempts, ee['attribution'], oe)
  683|         if type(oe.get('provisional')) is not bool or oe['provisional'] != want_provisional: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: provisional violates financial unresolved-billing predicate')
  684|         closed = [att_exp(attempts[at['attempt']]) for at in owned if attempts[at['attempt']]['obligation']['state'] in ('closed', 'none') and att_exp(attempts[at['attempt']])]
  685|         outst = [att_exp(attempts[at['attempt']]) for at in owned if attempts[at['attempt']]['obligation']['state'] == 'outstanding']
  686|         for label, items in (('owned_closed', closed), ('owned_outstanding', outst)):
  687|             want = hsum(items) if items else (0, 'exact', 0)
  688|             if esum_of(oe[label]) != want: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: {label} {esum_of(oe[label])} != sum of owned attempts {want}')
  689|         implied = set()
  690|         for at in owned:
  691|             implied |= set(attempts[at['attempt']]['blocking'])
  692|             if at['placement'] == 'unplaceable': implied.add('PlacementUnplaceable')
  693|             if at['placement'] in ('straddles_start', 'straddles_closure'): implied.add('PlacementStraddles')
  694|             if at['placement'] in ('before', 'after'): implied.add('AttributionContradiction')
  695|         for at in ee['attribution']:
  696|             if at['ownership'] in ('uncertain', 'unadmitted') and name in at.get('candidates', []) and attempts[at['attempt']]['exposure']['pool'] == 'pi_api':
  697|                 implied.add('OwnershipUncertain' if at['ownership'] == 'uncertain' else 'HistoricalUnadmitted'); implied |= set(attempts[at['attempt']]['blocking'])
  698|         scope = set(o['tree_sessions']) | {a['session'] for a in o['admissions']}
  699|         scope_alias = {s_['alias'] for s_ in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s_['session_id'] in scope}
  700|         for x in ex['non_attempt'] + ex['unreadable']:
  701|             if x['evidence'].split('/')[0] in scope_alias: implied |= set(x.get('blocking', []))
  702|         for x in ex['inventory']:
  703|             if x['session'] in scope_alias: implied |= set(x.get('blocking', []))
  704|         admitted_alias = {s_['alias'] for s_ in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s_['session_id'] in {a['session'] for a in o['admissions']}}
  705|         for alias in admitted_alias:
  706|             if ex['lifecycle'].get(alias, {}).get('reopened'): implied.add('ReopenedWithoutAdmission')     # R-10: persists after retirement
  707|         for line_ in ('owned_closed', 'owned_outstanding', 'predecessor_obligations'):
  708|             if oe[line_]['high']['kind'] == 'at_least': implied.add('UnresolvedExposure')
  709|         if closure_state(o, ref, c.get('profile')) == 'claimed': implied.add('ClosureNotEstablished')
  710|         adm_by_id = {a['admission_id']: a for a in o['admissions']}                      # commitments (12.3, Table G)
  711|         if sorted(cm['admission_id'] for cm in oe['commitments']) != sorted(adm_by_id): F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: commitments do not list every admission')
  712|         sessions_by_sid = {s_['session_id']: s_ for s_ in c['inputs']['sessions']}
  713|         for cm in oe['commitments']:
  714|             if cm['state'] not in COMMIT_STATES: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: bad commitment state'); continue
  715|             a = adm_by_id.get(cm['admission_id'])
  716|             if not a: continue
  717|             cap_rows = [r for r in a['rows'] if r.get('event') in ('pending', 'objective_admission')]
  718|             cap = cap_rows[0].get('max_usd') if cap_rows else None
  719|             alias = next((s_['alias'] for s_ in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s_['session_id'] == a['session']), None)
  720|             sp = hsum(spent.get(name, {}).get(alias, [])) if spent.get(name, {}).get(alias) else (0, 'exact', 0)
  721|             ok_cap = isinstance(cap, (int, float)) and not isinstance(cap, bool) and cap > 0
  722|             launched = any(r.get('event') in ('launched', 'objective_admission') for r in a['rows'])
  723|             released = any(r.get('event') == 'released' for r in a['rows'])
  724|             life = ex['lifecycle'].get(alias) if alias else None
  725|             capn = int(Decimal(str(cap)) * 10 ** 9) if ok_cap else None
  726|             gate = {'qualified': False, 'reasons': ['NoGateEvidence']}; contradicted = False
  727|             if not ok_cap: want = ('invalid', '0', 'exact')
  728|             elif released and not launched: want = ('released', '0', 'exact')
  729|             elif not launched:
  730|                 q, rs, contradicted = gate_qualification(c, o, a, 'bound', ref, ex['lifecycle'], sessions_by_sid, capn); gate = gate_projection(c, a, q, rs)
  731|                 want = ('pending', str(gate_qualification.ceiling if q else capn), 'exact' if q else 'at_least')
  732|             else:
  733|                 rem = str(max(0, capn - sp[2]))
  734|                 retired = life is not None and life['state'] == 'retired'; reopened = life is not None and life['reopened']
  735|                 if retired:
  736|                     q, rs, contradicted = gate_qualification(c, o, a, 'release', ref, ex['lifecycle'], sessions_by_sid); gate = gate_projection(c, a, q, rs)
  737|                     want = ('retired', '0', 'exact') if q else ('retired_resumable', rem, 'at_least')
  738|                 elif reopened:
  739|                     q, rs, contradicted = gate_qualification(c, o, a, 'release', ref, ex['lifecycle'], sessions_by_sid); gate = gate_projection(c, a, False, rs if not q else ['ReopenedWithoutAdmission'])
  740|                     want = ('reopened', rem, 'at_least')
  741|                 else:
  742|                     q, rs, contradicted = gate_qualification(c, o, a, 'bound', ref, ex['lifecycle'], sessions_by_sid, capn); gate = gate_projection(c, a, q, rs)
  743|                     want = ('launched_open', str(max(0, gate_qualification.ceiling - sp[2])) if q else rem, 'exact' if q else 'at_least')
  744|             got = (cm['state'], cm['committed'], cm.get('kind'))
  745|             if got != want: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: commitment {cm["admission_id"]} {got} != Table G {want} (A-2..A-5, OQ-1)')
  746|             if cm.get('gate') != gate: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: commitment {cm["admission_id"]} gate {cm.get("gate")} != G-1..G-7 {gate} (A9)')
  747|             if cm.get('kind') == 'at_least': implied.add('UnenforcedCommitment')
  748|             if cm['state'] == 'invalid': implied.add('CommitmentInvalid')
  749|             if contradicted: implied.add('GateContradicted')
  750|             # P5: a proxy high above a commitment is not a contradictory bill          # A-3: spend above cap
  751|         if set(oe['reasons']) != implied:
  752|             F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: reasons {sorted(oe["reasons"])} != implied by expected facts {sorted(implied)}')
  753|     for name, oe in exp_objs.items():                                                  # predecessor lines (12.2, R-07)
  754|         o = objs.get(name)
  755|         if not o: continue
  756|         if o.get('predecessor'):
  757|             p = exp_objs.get(o['predecessor'])
  758|             if not p: F.add(cid, f'predecessor {o["predecessor"]} has no expected result'); continue
  759|             unres = [att_exp(attempts[at['attempt']]) for at in ee['attribution'] if at['ownership'] == 'owned' and at['objective'] == o['predecessor']
  760|                      and att_exp(attempts[at['attempt']]) and attempts[at['attempt']]['exposure']['high']['kind'] == 'at_least']
  761|             com = [cm for cm in p['commitments'] if cm['state'] in OPEN_COMMIT]
  762|             kind = 'at_least' if unres or any(cm.get('kind') == 'at_least' for cm in com) else 'exact'
  763|             want = (0, kind, sum(x[2] for x in unres) + sum(nano(cm['committed']) for cm in com))
  764|             if esum_of(oe['predecessor_obligations']) != want:
  765|                 F.add(cid, f'{name}: predecessor_obligations {esum_of(oe["predecessor_obligations"])} != unresolved predecessor attempts + open commitments {want} (INV-A4, R-07)')
  766|         elif esum_of(oe['predecessor_obligations']) != (0, 'exact', 0): F.add(cid, f'{name}: predecessor line without a predecessor')
  767|     ad = ee.get('admission')                                                           # admission (12.4, 1.3-s1)
  768|     req = e['admission_request']
  769|     if ad:
  770|         o = objs[req['objective']]; oe = exp_objs[req['objective']]
  771|         if ad['new'] != req['new'] or ad['limit'] != o['limit'] or ad['objective'] != req['objective']: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: admission record does not match the request/limit')
  772|         ad_want, why = admission_decision(c, o, oe, req, ref)
  773|         if ad['decision'] != ad_want: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: admission {ad["decision"]} != 12.4 decision {ad_want} ({why})')
  774| 
  775| 
  776| def financial_provisional(attempts, attribution, objective):
  777|     """Bounded assertion over declared facts; no money reduction or lifecycle inference."""
  778|     def unresolved(a): return a['obligation']['state'] == 'outstanding' or a['exposure'].get('high', {}).get('kind') == 'at_least'
  779|     name = objective['objective']
  780|     return (any(unresolved(attempts[t['attempt']]) for t in attribution if t['ownership'] == 'owned' and t['objective'] == name)
  781|             or objective['predecessor_obligations']['high']['kind'] == 'at_least'
  782|             or any(cm['state'] != 'invalid' and cm.get('kind') == 'at_least' for cm in objective['commitments'])
  783|             or any(attempts[t['attempt']]['exposure']['pool'] == 'pi_api' and unresolved(attempts[t['attempt']]) for t in attribution
  784|                    if t['ownership'] in ('uncertain', 'unadmitted') and name in t.get('candidates', [])))
  785| 
  786| 
  787| def admission_decision(c, o, oe, req, ref):
  788|     """Contract 12.4 (1.3-s1): closure, limit, known over-limit (OQ-2), accounting, headroom."""
  789|     cs = closure_state(o, ref, c.get('profile'))
  790|     if cs == 'closed': return 'refused', 'established closure (OQ-4)'
  791|     if cs == 'claimed': return 'not_established', 'claimed closure not established (OQ-4)'
  792|     lim = o['limit']
  793|     if not (isinstance(lim, str) and re.fullmatch(r'\d+', lim)): return 'not_established', 'limit not established'
  794|     known = sum(nano(oe[l]['high']['nano']) for l in ('owned_closed', 'owned_outstanding', 'predecessor_obligations')) + \
  795|         sum(nano(cm['committed']) for cm in oe['commitments'] if cm['state'] != 'invalid') + nano(req['new'])
  796|     if known > nano(lim): return 'refused', f'known {known} > limit {lim} (OQ-2)'
  797|     if oe['status'] == 'not_established': return 'not_established', 'known within limit, unknown may exceed'
  798|     return 'admitted', 'headroom'
  799| 
  800| 
  801| def corpus_cases(corpus):
  802|     out = []
  803|     for p in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(corpus, 'cases', '*.json'))):
  804|         c = json.load(open(p))
  805|         if os.path.basename(p)[:-5] != c.get('case_id'): print(f'FAIL {p}: file name != case_id')
  806|         out.append(c)
  807|     return out
  808| 
  809| 
  810| def first_decision(c): return (c['evaluations'][0]['expected'].get('admission') or {}).get('decision')
  811| 
  812| 
  813| def valid_admitted(cases, profile=None):
  814|     return [c for c in cases if c['role'] in ('valid_control', 'control') and first_decision(c) == 'admitted' and not any(a['blocking'] for a in c['expected']['attempts'])
  815|             and (profile is None or c.get('profile') == profile)]
  816| 
  817| 
  818| def check_corpus(corpus, rates_dir):
  819|     rates = load_rates(rates_dir); F = Failures(); cases = corpus_cases(corpus)
  820|     ids = [c['case_id'] for c in cases]
  821|     for i in {x for x in ids if ids.count(x) > 1}: F.add(i, 'duplicate case id')
  822|     by = {c['case_id']: c for c in cases}
  823|     seen_inputs = {}
  824|     for c in cases:
  825|         try: check_case(c, rates, F)
  826|         except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, StopIteration) as e: F.add(c.get('case_id', '?'), f'structure error: {type(e).__name__} {e}')
  827|         for ctl in c.get('controls', []):
  828|             if ctl not in by: F.add(c['case_id'], f'control {ctl} does not exist')
  829|         dig = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps([c['inputs'], [(e['objectives'], e['admission_request']) for e in c['evaluations']]], sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
  830|         if dig in seen_inputs:
  831|             other = by[seen_inputs[dig]]
  832|             same = json.dumps([other['expected'], [e['expected'] for e in other['evaluations']]], sort_keys=True) == \
  833|                 json.dumps([c['expected'], [e['expected'] for e in c['evaluations']]], sort_keys=True)
  834|             F.add(c['case_id'], f'same inputs as {seen_inputs[dig]} ({"duplicate" if same else "CONFLICTING expectations"})')
  835|         seen_inputs[dig] = c['case_id']
  836|         h = c.get('historical') or {}
  837|         if 'expected' in h: F.add(c['case_id'], 'historical block contains normative fields')
  838|         a134 = h.get('a134_086bdec1')
  839|         if a134 and first_decision(c) and a134.get('decision') != first_decision(c) and not c.get('rationale'):
  840|             F.add(c['case_id'], 'historical A1.3.4 decision differs from the normative one without a rationale')
  841|         pair = c.get('evidence_pair')
  842|         if pair:                                                                       # INV-A5 (R-11)
  843|             other = by.get(pair.get('other'))
  844|             if not other or pair.get('relation') not in ('subset', 'superset') or not isinstance(pair.get('permitted_release'), bool):
  845|                 F.add(c['case_id'], 'bad evidence_pair'); continue
  846|             more, less = (other, c) if pair['relation'] == 'subset' else (c, other)
  847|             lm = {s['alias']: s for s in more['inputs']['sessions']}; ll = {s['alias']: s for s in less['inputs']['sessions']}
  848|             if not set(ll) <= set(lm): F.add(c['case_id'], 'evidence_pair: sessions are not a subset'); continue
  849|             def contained(small, big): return all(small.count(x) <= big.count(x) for x in small)
  850|             if not all(contained(ll[a_]['lifecycle'], lm[a_]['lifecycle']) and all(t in lm[a_]['logs'] and contained(log['lines'], lm[a_]['logs'][t]['lines'])
  851|                                                                                      for t, log in ll[a_]['logs'].items()) for a_ in ll):
  852|                 F.add(c['case_id'], 'evidence_pair: the smaller case holds evidence the larger one lacks, so it is not a subset (INV-A5)'); continue
  853|             if pair['permitted_release']:
  854|                 gated = more.get('profile') == 'future_qualified_gate' and all(cm.get('gate', {}).get('qualified') for e_ in more['evaluations']
  855|                                                                                 for o_ in e_['expected']['objectives'] for cm in o_['commitments'] if cm['state'] == 'retired')
  856|                 if not gated or not any(cm['state'] == 'retired' for e_ in more['evaluations'] for o_ in e_['expected']['objectives'] for cm in o_['commitments']):
  857|                     F.add(c['case_id'], 'evidence_pair: a permitted release needs a retirement with a qualified gate (A5, OQ-1)')
  858|                 logs_same = all(lm[a_]['logs'] == ll[a_]['logs'] for a_ in ll)
  859|                 extra = [r for a_ in ll for r in lm[a_]['lifecycle'] if r not in ll[a_]['lifecycle']]
  860|                 if not logs_same or not extra or any(r['event'] != 'retire' or not r.get('at') for r in extra):
  861|                     F.add(c['case_id'], 'evidence_pair: a permitted release must add only dated retire rows (A5 exception)')
  862|             elif RANK[first_decision(more)] < RANK[first_decision(less)]:
  863|                 F.add(c['case_id'], f'evidence_pair: added non-settlement evidence turned a denial into admission ({first_decision(less)} -> {first_decision(more)}) (INV-A5)')
  864|     for inv in INVARIANTS:
  865|         if not any(inv in c['invariants'] for c in cases): F.add('coverage', f'invariant {inv} covered by no case')
  866|     for cl in CLASSES:
  867|         if not any(c['class'] == cl for c in cases): F.add('coverage', f'no case of class {cl}')
  868|     va = valid_admitted(cases)
  869|     blocked = [c for c in cases if any((e['expected'].get('admission') or {}).get('decision') == 'not_established' for e in c['evaluations'])]
  870|     if len(va) < MIN_VALID_ADMITTED: F.add('coverage', f'only {len(va)} admitted valid controls (< {MIN_VALID_ADMITTED})')
  871|     for prof, mn in MIN_ADMITTED.items():
  872|         n = len(valid_admitted(cases, prof))
  873|         if n < mn: F.add('coverage', f'profile {prof}: only {n} admitted valid controls (< {mn})')
  874|     metered = [c for c in valid_admitted(cases, 'future_qualified_gate') if any(a['exposure'].get('pool') == 'pi_api' for a in c['expected']['attempts'])]
  875|     if len(metered) < MIN_ADMITTED['future_qualified_gate']: F.add('coverage', f'only {len(metered)} future-gated admitted controls with metered attempts')
  876|     if len(blocked) < MIN_BLOCKED: F.add('coverage', f'only {len(blocked)} blocking cases (< {MIN_BLOCKED})')
  877|     for c in cases:
  878|         if c.get('twin_of'):
  879|             t_ = by.get(c['twin_of'])
  880|             if not t_: F.add(c['case_id'], 'twin_of names no case'); continue
  881|             norm = lambda x: sorted(x['inputs']['sessions'], key=lambda s_: s_['alias'])
  882|             if norm(c) != norm(t_) or canon(c['expected']) != canon(t_['expected']) or canon([e['expected'] for e in c['evaluations']]) != canon([e['expected'] for e in t_['evaluations']]):
  883|                 F.add(c['case_id'], f'twin of {c["twin_of"]} differs beyond presentation order (INV-F8)')
  884|     if not [c for c in cases if len(c['evaluations']) > 1]: F.add('coverage', 'no multi-window case (INV-F7)')
  885|     return F, cases
  886| 
  887| 
  888| # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ revision log
  889| ABSENT = '__absent__'
  890| 
  891| 
  892| def _locate(c, field):
  893|     """Container and key for a revision field path (revise_rev2.py, revise_rev3.py)."""
  894|     if field.startswith('attempts['):
  895|         key, rest = field[len('attempts['):].split('].', 1)
  896|         node = next(a for a in c['expected']['attempts'] if a['key'] == key)
  897|     elif field.startswith('lifecycle['):
  898|         alias, rest = field[len('lifecycle['):].split('].', 1)
  899|         node = c['expected']['lifecycle'][alias]
  900|     elif '/' not in field:                                    # top-level case fields (revision 4: profile, evidence_pair)
  901|         node, rest = c, field
  902|     else:
  903|         ev_name, rest = field.split('/', 1)
  904|         ee = next(e for e in c['evaluations'] if e['name'] == ev_name)['expected']
  905|         if rest.startswith('attribution['):
  906|             key, rest = rest[len('attribution['):].split('].', 1)
  907|             node = next(at for at in ee['attribution'] if at['attempt'] == key)
  908|         elif rest.startswith('admission.'):
  909|             node, rest = ee['admission'], rest[len('admission.'):]
  910|         else:
  911|             obj, rest = rest.split('.', 1)
  912|             node = next(o for o in ee['objectives'] if o['objective'] == obj)
  913|             m = re.match(r'commitments\[([^\]]+)\]\.(.*)$', rest)
  914|             if m: node, rest = next(cm for cm in node['commitments'] if cm['admission_id'] == m.group(1)), m.group(2)
  915|     parts = rest.split('.')
  916|     for p_ in parts[:-1]: node = node[p_]
  917|     return node, parts[-1]
  918| 
  919| 
  920| R5_ADDED = ('contract', 'revisions_r7')
  921| 
  922| 
  923| def check_revisions(corpus, previous):
  924|     """Undo every revision-6 entry of each case and require the revision-5 case back exactly (apart from the contract
  925|     string, the revision-6 log and an appended rationale). Added cases must be marked added_in 1.5-s1. Historical
  926|     blocks and the earlier logs must be unchanged."""
  927|     F = Failures(); prev = {c['case_id']: c for c in corpus_cases(previous)}; cur = corpus_cases(corpus)
  928|     for c in cur:
  929|         cid = c['case_id']
  930|         if cid not in prev:
  931|             if c.get('added_in') != '1.6-s1': F.add(cid, 'not in the previous corpus and not marked added_in 1.6-s1')
  932|             continue
  933|         p = prev[cid]
  934|         if p.get('contract') != PREVIOUS_CONTRACT: F.add(cid, 'previous case is not revision 6')
  935|         u = copy.deepcopy(c)
  936|         for r in reversed(u.get('revisions_r7', [])):
  937|             try:
  938|                 node, k = _locate(u, r['field'])
  939|                 if node.get(k, ABSENT) != r['to']: F.add(cid, f'revision {r["field"]}: current value is not its logged "to"')
  940|                 if r['from'] == ABSENT: node.pop(k, None)
  941|                 else: node[k] = r['from']
  942|             except (StopIteration, KeyError, ValueError) as e_: F.add(cid, f'revision {r["field"]} does not resolve: {e_}')
  943|         for k in R5_ADDED: u.pop(k, None)
  944|         q = {k: v for k, v in p.items() if k not in R5_ADDED}
  945|         if canon(u) != canon(q):
  946|             F.add(cid, f'differs from the previous case beyond its logged revisions in {sorted(k for k in set(u) | set(q) if canon(u.get(k)) != canon(q.get(k)))}')
  947|         if canon(c['historical']) != canon(p['historical']): F.add(cid, 'historical outcome changed')
  948|         if p.get('rationale') and not (c.get('rationale') or '').startswith(p['rationale']): F.add(cid, 'previous rationale not preserved')
  949|     curids = {c['case_id'] for c in cur}
  950|     for cid in prev:
  951|         if cid not in curids: F.add(cid, 'previous case missing from the revised corpus')
  952|     fp = {f for c in prev.values() for f in c['findings']}; fc = {f for c in cur for f in c['findings']}
  953|     if fp - fc: F.add('coverage', f'findings lost: {sorted(fp - fc)}')
  954|     return F, len(prev), len(cur), len(fp), len(fc)
  955| 
  956| 
  957| # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ grading result sets
  958| def expected_results(cases):
  959|     """The corpus's own expectations in result-set form (one entry per evaluation)."""
  960|     out = {}
  961|     for c in cases:
  962|         ex = c['expected']
  963|         # each evaluation gets its own copy: a shared `attempts` object would hide window-dependent defects (P1.30 fix)
  964|         out[c['case_id']] = {'evaluations': {e['name']: copy.deepcopy({'attempts': ex['attempts'], 'non_attempt': ex['non_attempt'], 'unreadable': ex['unreadable'],
  965|                                                                         'inventory': ex['inventory'], 'lifecycle': ex['lifecycle'], **e['expected']})
  966|                                               for e in c['evaluations']}}
  967|     return out
  968| 
  969| 
  970| def canon(x):
  971|     if isinstance(x, dict): return {k: canon(v) for k, v in sorted(x.items())}
  972|     if isinstance(x, list): return sorted((canon(v) for v in x), key=lambda v: json.dumps(v, sort_keys=True))
  973|     return x
  974| 
  975| 
  976| GRADED = ('attempts', 'non_attempt', 'unreadable', 'inventory', 'lifecycle', 'attribution', 'objectives', 'admission')
  977| S2A_GRADED = ('attempts',)
  978| 
  979| 
  980| def s2a_subset(cases):
  981|     """P6 input-only current-producer scope; missing-launch dollar evidence stays an unresolved negative control.
  982|     Selection does not establish E admissibility, correlation, lane identity or producer reachability."""
  983|     out = []
  984|     for c in cases:
  985|         if c.get('profile') != 'current_producer': continue
  986|         selected = False
  987|         for session in c['inputs']['sessions']:
  988|             launch = session.get('launch')
  989|             if launch is not None and launch.get('auth_lane') != 'api': continue
  990|             for log in session.get('logs', {}).values():
  991|                 for line in log['lines']:
  992|                     row = parse(line)
  993|                     if isinstance(row, dict) and (row.get('event') == 'usd_reserved' or 'usd' in row): selected = True
  994|         if selected: out.append(c)
  995|     return out
  996| 
  997| 
  998| def grade(cases, results, fields=GRADED):
  999|     """Exact equality per graded field: a result more restrictive than the expectation differs like any other."""
 1000|     F = Failures(); diff_fields = {}
 1001|     for c in cases:
 1002|         r = results.get(c['case_id'])
 1003|         if r is None: F.add(c['case_id'], 'no result'); continue
 1004|         exp = expected_results([c])[c['case_id']]['evaluations']
 1005|         evs = r.get('evaluations', {})
 1006|         first = None
 1007|         for name, want in exp.items():
 1008|             got = evs.get(name)
 1009|             if got is None: F.add(c['case_id'], f'{name}: no result'); continue
 1010|             for f in fields:
 1011|                 if canon(got.get(f)) != canon(want.get(f)): F.add(c['case_id'], f'{name}: {f} differs'); diff_fields[f] = diff_fields.get(f, 0) + 1
 1012|             facts = canon([got.get('attempts'), got.get('non_attempt'), got.get('unreadable')])
 1013|             if first is None: first = facts
 1014|             elif facts != first: F.add(c['case_id'], f'{name}: attempt facts change with the objective window (INV-F7)'); diff_fields['window'] = diff_fields.get('window', 0) + 1
 1015|     return F, diff_fields
 1016| 
 1017| 
 1018| # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ self-tests
 1019| def walk_money(obj, fn):
 1020|     if isinstance(obj, dict):
 1021|         for k, v in list(obj.items()):
 1022|             if k in ('nano', 'low', 'high', 'committed') and isinstance(v, str) and re.fullmatch(r'-?\d+', v): obj[k] = fn(v)
 1023|             else: walk_money(v, fn)
 1024|     elif isinstance(obj, list):
 1025|         for v in obj: walk_money(v, fn)
 1026| 
 1027| 
 1028| def defects(cases):
 1029|     """Defective result sets, each with the graded field it must be caught in (its intended reason)."""
 1030|     base = expected_results(cases)
 1031|     by = {c['case_id']: c for c in cases}
 1032|     def each(fn):
 1033|         d = copy.deepcopy(base)
 1034|         for cid, r in d.items():
 1035|             for name, ev in r['evaluations'].items(): fn(cid, name, ev)
 1036|         return d
 1037|     def restatus(o): o['status'] = 'not_established' if o['reasons'] else 'established'
 1038|     def accept(cid, name, ev):
 1039|         if ev.get('admission'): ev['admission']['decision'] = 'admitted'
 1040|         for o in ev['objectives']: o['status'], o['reasons'] = 'established', []
 1041|         for a in ev['attempts']: a['blocking'] = []
 1042|     def reject(cid, name, ev):
 1043|         if ev.get('admission'): ev['admission']['decision'] = 'not_established'
 1044|         for o in ev['objectives']: o['status'] = 'not_established'; o['reasons'] = sorted(set(o['reasons']) | {'UnresolvedExposure'})
 1045|     def zero(cid, name, ev): walk_money(ev, lambda v: '0')
 1046|     def unknown(cid, name, ev):
 1047|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1048|             if a['exposure'].get('pool') == 'pi_api': a['exposure']['high']['kind'] = 'at_least'
 1049|     def drop_malformed(cid, name, ev):
 1050|         ev['unreadable'] = []; ev['non_attempt'] = [x for x in ev['non_attempt'] if not x.get('blocking')]
 1051|         ev['attempts'] = [a for a in ev['attempts'] if a['correlation']['kind'] not in ('unattributed', 'ambiguous')]
 1052|     def settle_to_res(cid, name, ev):
 1053|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1054|             s = a['settlement']
 1055|             if s.get('decision') == 'valid' and s.get('basis') == 'usage' and 'reserved' in s:
 1056|                 a['exposure']['low'] = {'nano': '0', 'basis': 'none'}; a['exposure']['high'] = {'kind': 'exact', 'nano': s['reserved'], 'basis': 'proxy_reservation'}
 1057|     def no_diag(cid, name, ev):
 1058|         for a in ev['attempts']: a['diagnostics'] = []
 1059|     first_names = {cid: next(iter(r['evaluations'])) for cid, r in base.items()}
 1060|     def window_money(cid, name, ev):
 1061|         if name != first_names[cid] and ev['attempts']:
 1062|             for a in ev['attempts']:
 1063|                 if a['exposure'].get('pool') == 'pi_api':
 1064|                     a['exposure']['high']['nano'] = str(int(a['exposure']['high']['nano']) + 1); break
 1065|     def unknown_as_exact(cid, name, ev):
 1066|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1067|             if a['exposure'].get('pool') == 'pi_api': a['exposure']['high']['kind'] = 'exact'
 1068|     def unresolved_as_zero(cid, name, ev):
 1069|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1070|             if a['exposure'].get('pool') == 'pi_api' and a['exposure']['high']['kind'] == 'at_least': a['exposure']['high'] = {'kind': 'exact', 'nano': '0', 'basis': 'none'}
 1071|     def lose_obligations(cid, name, ev):
 1072|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1073|             if a['obligation']['state'] == 'outstanding': a['obligation'] = {'state': 'closed', 'amount_source': 'reservation_confirmed'}
 1074|         for o in ev['objectives']: o['owned_outstanding'] = {'low': '0', 'high': {'kind': 'exact', 'nano': '0'}}
 1075|     def double_count(cid, name, ev):
 1076|         for o in ev['objectives']:
 1077|             oc = o['owned_closed']; oc['low'] = str(2 * int(oc['low'])); oc['high']['nano'] = str(2 * int(oc['high']['nano']))
 1078|     def early_release(cid, name, ev):
 1079|         for o in ev['objectives']:
 1080|             for cm in o['commitments']:
 1081|                 if cm['state'] in OPEN_COMMIT: cm['state'], cm['committed'], cm['kind'] = 'released', '0', 'exact'
 1082|     def diagnostics_move_money(cid, name, ev):
 1083|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1084|             if a['diagnostics'] and a['exposure'].get('pool') == 'pi_api': a['exposure']['low'] = {'nano': '0', 'basis': 'none'}; a['exposure']['high']['nano'] = str(int(a['exposure']['high']['nano']) + 1)
 1085|     def unadmitted_ignored(cid, name, ev):
 1086|         for at in ev['attribution']:
 1087|             if at['ownership'] == 'unadmitted': at['ownership'] = 'unowned'; at['candidates'] = []
 1088|         for o in ev['objectives']: o['reasons'] = [r for r in o['reasons'] if r != 'HistoricalUnadmitted']; restatus(o)
 1089|     def forwarding_unknown(cid, name, ev):
 1090|         for a in ev['attempts']: a['forwarding'] = 'unknown'
 1091|     # revision-3 defect classes (P1.30)
 1092|     def lost_predecessor(cid, name, ev):            # R-07: closed-but-unresolved predecessor spend dropped from the successor line
 1093|         for o in ev['objectives']:
 1094|             pl = o['predecessor_obligations']
 1095|             if pl['high']['nano'] != '0' or pl['high']['kind'] == 'at_least':
 1096|                 o['predecessor_obligations'] = {'low': '0', 'high': {'kind': 'exact', 'nano': '0'}}
 1097|                 o['reasons'] = [r for r in o['reasons'] if r != 'UnresolvedExposure']; restatus(o)
 1098|                 if ev.get('admission') and ev['admission']['objective'] == o['objective'] and o['status'] == 'established': ev['admission']['decision'] = 'admitted'
 1099|     def erased_contradiction(cid, name, ev):        # R-08: admitting objectives filtered out of an Uncertain attempt's candidates
 1100|         for at in ev['attribution']:
 1101|             if at['ownership'] == 'uncertain': at['candidates'] = []
 1102|         for o in ev['objectives']: o['reasons'] = [r for r in o['reasons'] if r != 'OwnershipUncertain']; restatus(o)
 1103|     def closure_after_headroom(cid, name, ev):      # Q11/R-09: closure evaluated after accounting and headroom
 1104|         c = by[cid]; e = next(x for x in c['evaluations'] if x['name'] == name)
 1105|         if ev.get('admission'):
 1106|             o = next(x for x in e['objectives'] if x['objective'] == ev['admission']['objective'])
 1107|             if closure_state(o, c['inputs']['reference_time'], c.get('profile')) == 'closed':
 1108|                 oe = next(x for x in ev['objectives'] if x['objective'] == o['objective'])
 1109|                 ev['admission']['decision'] = 'not_established' if oe['status'] == 'not_established' else 'admitted'
 1110|     def timestamp_only(cid, name, ev):              # R-12: candidates from isolated row stamps
 1111|         c = by[cid]; e = next(x for x in c['evaluations'] if x['name'] == name)
 1112|         atts = {a['key']: a for a in ev['attempts']}; lines, _ = source_lines(c)
 1113|         for at in ev['attribution']:
 1114|             if at['ownership'] != 'unadmitted': continue
 1115|             stamps = [parse(lines.get(r)).get('at') for r in atts[at['attempt']]['evidence'] if parse(lines.get(r)) and isinstance(parse(lines.get(r)).get('at'), str)]
 1116|             if stamps: at['candidates'] = [o['objective'] for o in e['objectives'] if o['objective'] in at['candidates'] and max(stamps) >= o['started_at']]
 1117|         for o in ev['objectives']:
 1118|             if not any(o['objective'] in at.get('candidates', []) for at in ev['attribution'] if at['ownership'] == 'unadmitted'):
 1119|                 o['reasons'] = [r for r in o['reasons'] if r != 'HistoricalUnadmitted']; restatus(o)
 1120|     def false_finite_tier(cid, name, ev):           # R-03: a missing or unrecognised tier priced as a finite range
 1121|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1122|             v = a['valuation']
 1123|             if v.get('state') == 'unpriceable' and 'TierUnestablished' in v.get('reasons', []):
 1124|                 a['exposure']['high']['kind'] = 'exact'; a['billing'] = 'established'; a['blocking'] = [b for b in a['blocking'] if b != 'UnresolvedExposure']
 1125|     def cap_as_bound(cid, name, ev):                # R-04: an unenforced cap counted as a finite commitment
 1126|         for o in ev['objectives']:
 1127|             hit = False
 1128|             for cm in o['commitments']:
 1129|                 if cm.get('kind') == 'at_least': cm['kind'] = 'exact'; hit = True
 1130|             if hit: o['reasons'] = [r for r in o['reasons'] if r != 'UnenforcedCommitment']; restatus(o)
 1131|     def billing_from_obligation(cid, name, ev):     # R-07/F12: billing derived from the obligation state
 1132|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1133|             if a.get('billing') != 'not_applicable': a['billing'] = 'established' if a['obligation']['state'] == 'closed' else 'unresolved'
 1134|     def retired_released(cid, name, ev):            # OQ-1: a current-producer retirement treated as a release
 1135|         if by[cid].get('profile') != 'current_producer': return
 1136|         for o in ev['objectives']:
 1137|             for cm in o['commitments']:
 1138|                 if cm['state'] == 'retired_resumable': cm.update(state='retired', committed='0', kind='exact')
 1139|     def self_asserted_trusted(cid, name, ev):       # OQ-1: an unqualified gate claim trusted
 1140|         for o in ev['objectives']:
 1141|             for cm in o['commitments']:
 1142|                 if cm.get('gate', {}).get('reasons') and cm['gate']['reasons'] != ['NoGateEvidence']: cm['gate'] = {'qualified': True, 'reasons': []}
 1143|     def gate_bound_ignored(cid, name, ev):          # OQ-1/OQ-3: a qualified billing bound treated as unenforced
 1144|         for o in ev['objectives']:
 1145|             for cm in o['commitments']:
 1146|                 if cm['state'] == 'launched_open' and cm.get('kind') == 'exact': cm['kind'] = 'at_least'
 1147|     def known_over_not_refused(cid, name, ev):      # OQ-2: refusal by known amounts reported as not_established
 1148|         c = by[cid]; e = next(x for x in c['evaluations'] if x['name'] == name)
 1149|         if ev.get('admission') and ev['admission']['decision'] == 'refused':
 1150|             o = next(x for x in e['objectives'] if x['objective'] == ev['admission']['objective'])
 1151|             if closure_state(o, c['inputs']['reference_time'], c.get('profile')) == 'open': ev['admission']['decision'] = 'not_established'
 1152|     def unknown_below_refused(cid, name, ev):       # OQ-2: unknown exposure below the limit reported as refusal
 1153|         if ev.get('admission') and ev['admission']['decision'] == 'not_established': ev['admission']['decision'] = 'refused'
 1154|     def release_incomplete(cid, name, ev):          # A-01: an ineligible usage settlement replaces the reservation
 1155|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1156|             if a.get('replacement', {}).get('reason') in ('UsageIncomplete', 'UsageUnpriceable'):
 1157|                 a['replacement'] = {'eligible': True, 'reason': 'UsageBound'}; a['obligation'] = {'state': 'closed', 'amount_source': 'settlement_amount'}
 1158|                 v = a['valuation']; vh = v.get('high') or v.get('priced_high') or a['settlement'].get('charged', '0')
 1159|                 a['exposure']['high'] = {'kind': 'at_least', 'nano': str(max(int(vh), int(a['settlement'].get('charged', '0')))), 'basis': 'usage_valuation'}
 1160|     def discard_usage(cid, name, ev):               # A-02: error status drops its supported usage
 1161|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1162|             if a['settlement'].get('basis') == 'error_status' and a['valuation'].get('state') == 'valued':
 1163|                 a['exposure'] = {'pool': 'pi_api', 'low': {'nano': '0', 'basis': 'none'}, 'high': {'kind': 'at_least', 'nano': a['settlement']['reserved'], 'basis': 'proxy_reservation'}}
 1164|     def finite_from_incomplete(cid, name, ev):      # A-01/FB-1: incomplete or unpriceable usage made a finite high
 1165|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1166|             if a.get('replacement', {}).get('reason') in ('UsageIncomplete', 'UsageUnpriceable'): a['exposure']['high']['kind'] = 'exact'; a['billing'] = 'established'
 1167|     def fake_bound_qualified(cid, name, ev):        # A-03: an unbound or mis-bound billing gate treated as qualified
 1168|         for o in ev['objectives']:
 1169|             for cm in o['commitments']:
 1170|                 if any(r.startswith('GateBound') for r in cm.get('gate', {}).get('reasons', [])): cm['gate'] = {'qualified': True, 'reasons': []}; cm['kind'] = 'exact'
 1171|     def reservations_summed(cid, name, ev):         # A-09: conflicting reservations summed
 1172|         if cid == 'R04-reused-reservation-id':
 1173|             for a in ev['attempts']: a['exposure']['high']['nano'] = '900000000'
 1174|     def more_restrictive(cid, name, ev):            # a result stricter than the expectation must still fail
 1175|         for a in ev['attempts']:
 1176|             if a['exposure'].get('pool') == 'pi_api' and 'UnresolvedExposure' not in a['blocking']: a['blocking'] = sorted(a['blocking'] + ['UnresolvedExposure'])
 1177|     return {'always_accept': (each(accept), 'admission'), 'always_reject': (each(reject), 'admission'), 'always_zero': (each(zero), 'attempts'),
 1178|             'always_unknown': (each(unknown), 'attempts'), 'drop_malformed': (each(drop_malformed), 'attempts'),
 1179|             'settlement_replaced_by_reservation': (each(settle_to_res), 'attempts'), 'suppress_diagnostics': (each(no_diag), 'attempts'),

 1184|             'forwarding_always_unknown': (each(forwarding_unknown), 'attempts'),
 1185|             'lost_predecessor_obligations': (each(lost_predecessor), 'objectives'), 'erased_admission_contradiction': (each(erased_contradiction), 'attribution'),
