 1188|             'billing_from_obligation': (each(billing_from_obligation), 'attempts'), 'more_restrictive_than_expected': (each(more_restrictive), 'attempts'),
 1189|             'current_retirement_released': (each(retired_released), 'objectives'), 'self_asserted_gate_trusted': (each(self_asserted_trusted), 'objectives'),
 1190|             'qualified_billing_bound_ignored': (each(gate_bound_ignored), 'objectives'), 'known_over_limit_not_refused': (each(known_over_not_refused), 'admission'),
 1191|             'unknown_below_limit_refused': (each(unknown_below_refused), 'admission'),
 1192|             'incomplete_usage_releases_reservation': (each(release_incomplete), 'attempts'), 'error_status_discards_usage': (each(discard_usage), 'attempts'),
 1193|             'finite_high_from_incomplete_pricing': (each(finite_from_incomplete), 'attempts'), 'fake_billing_bound_qualified': (each(fake_bound_qualified), 'objectives'),
 1194|             'conflicting_reservations_summed': (each(reservations_summed), 'attempts')}
 1195| 
 1196| 
 1197| def selftest(corpus, rates_dir):
 1198|     F, cases = check_corpus(corpus, rates_dir)
 1199|     report = {'corpus_failures': len(F)}
 1200|     ok = not F
 1201|     good, _ = grade(cases, expected_results(cases))
 1202|     report['correct_result_set_failures'] = len(good)
 1203|     ok &= not good
 1204|     for name, (d, field) in defects(cases).items():
 1205|         fails, fields = grade(cases, d)
 1206|         caught = len(fails) > 0 and fields.get(field, 0) > 0
 1207|         report[name] = {'differences': len(fails), 'intended_field': field, 'intended_field_differences': fields.get(field, 0), 'caught_for_intended_reason': caught}
 1208|         ok &= caught
 1209|     print(json.dumps(report, indent=1, sort_keys=True))
 1210|     return ok
 1211| 
 1212| 
 1213| def main(argv):
 1214|     if len(argv) < 3: print(__doc__); return 2
 1215|     cmd = argv[1]
 1216|     if cmd == 'check':
 1217|         F, cases = check_corpus(argv[2], argv[3])
 1218|         for f in F: print('FAIL', f)
 1219|         by = {p_: len([c for c in cases if c.get('profile') == p_]) for p_ in sorted(PROFILES)}
 1220|         adm = {p_: len(valid_admitted(cases, p_)) for p_ in sorted(PROFILES)}
 1221|         print(f'{len(cases)} cases {by}, admitted valid controls {adm}, {len(F)} failures'); return 1 if F else 0
 1222|     if cmd == 'results':
 1223|         cases = corpus_cases(argv[2]); fields = GRADED
 1224|         if '--scope' in argv and argv[argv.index('--scope') + 1] == 's2a': cases, fields = s2a_subset(cases), S2A_GRADED
 1225|         F, _ = grade(cases, json.load(open(argv[3])), fields)
 1226|         for f in F: print('FAIL', f)
 1227|         print(f'{len(cases)} cases graded, {len(F)} differences'); return 1 if F else 0
 1228|     if cmd == 'selftest':
 1229|         return 0 if selftest(argv[2], argv[3]) else 1
 1230|     if cmd == 'revisions':
 1231|         F, np_, nc, fp, fc = check_revisions(argv[2], argv[3])
 1232|         for f in F: print('FAIL', f)
 1233|         print(f'previous {np_} cases / {fp} finding tags; current {nc} cases / {fc} finding tags; {len(F)} failures'); return 1 if F else 0
 1234|     if cmd == 's2a-subset':
 1235|         sub = s2a_subset(corpus_cases(argv[2]))
 1236|         for c in sub: print(c['case_id'])
 1237|         print(f'{len(sub)} cases'); return 0
 1238|     print(__doc__); return 2
 1239| 
 1240| 
 1241| if __name__ == '__main__':
 1242|     sys.exit(main(sys.argv))
===== END FILE validator/validate_corpus.py =====

===== BEGIN FILE current-review/R7A-response.md | sha256 b4b1ed688ceac5574eeced36936d5754dfb7e588334c93842d6b9559ce1e67f2 | 11599 bytes | 135 lines =====
    1| {"module":"A","verdict":"CHANGES_REQUIRED","module_complete":true,"s2a_readiness":"NOT_READY","p1_count":2,"p2_count":7,"p1_touching_CK":2,"architecture_assessment":"design_review_required","limits":["All 83 file boundaries and the end sentinel are present; hashes and byte counts were not independently verified.","Read-only review without tools or test execution.","Only 50/175 cases are supplied; evaluations, complete frozen audit facts, inventory entries and predecessor bytes are omitted.","Legacy validator implementation and complete sabotage output are unavailable; reported passes do not establish semantic correctness.","Raw-axis qualification is not complete raw-to-output conformance.","Trusted-fixture positives establish conditional mock policy only; live verification and enforcement remain unestablished."]}
    2| 
    3| ## Findings
    4| 
    5| ### A-01 — P1: Adding a reservation can halve retained aggregate exposure
    6| 
    7| **Location:** `spec/ck.json:2`, `CORRELATION C-2`, `X-BRANCH-022`, `X-BRANCH-027`, `X-COMPONENTS`, `INV-F2`, `INV-F3`.
    8| 
    9| **Counterexample:** Begin with two distinct request settlements in one source, both naming `usd.rid=1`, each claiming a well-formed 500M charge, with reservation basis and no reported usage. No reservation row exists. C-2 assigns separate `Row` keys; each rejected orphan contributes `AtLeast(500M)`, totaling `AtLeast(1000M)`.
   10| 
   11| Append a 500M reservation with that rid. C-2 now joins both settlements into one reservation group. Their decision is `Conflicting`; X-27 takes `max(R, claimed charges)=500M`. Aggregate exposure becomes `AtLeast(500M)`.
   12| 
   13| The added evidence is not an eligible usage settlement. This violates I-1 and INV-F3 despite every individual X branch retaining its available reservation.
   14| 
   15| **Consequence:** Per-tuple declaration coverage does not protect exposure across correlation changes. The C identity/merge-to-X aggregation boundary needs design review.
   16| 
   17| **Origin:** Unestablished; demonstrated directly from current rules.
   18| 
   19| ### A-02 — P1: Qualified expectations erase supported usage contrary to X’s owning result
   20| 
   21| **Location:** `spec/ck.json:2`, `X-BRANCH-025`; `cases/A06-reservation-usage-null-status-no-id.json:2`, `cases/P133-A09-conflicting-reservations-with-settlement.json:2`, `cases/P130-R05-conflicting-keeps-valuation-high.json:2`, `cases/V01-cheaper-response-model-undercharge.json:2`.
   22| 
   23| **Counterexamples:** X-25 requires `low=usage_low` for rejected/conflicting settlements with valued usage. Nevertheless:
   24| 
   25| - A06 and P133-A09 record valuation low 15M but exposure low zero.
   26| - P130-R05 records valuation low 20M but exposure low zero.
   27| - V01-undercharge records valuation low 60M but exposure low zero.
   28| 
   29| For A06, independent arithmetic is `1000×10000 + 100×50000 = 15M` nano.
   30| 
   31| `tooling/tests/independent_raw_axes.rs:429–447` compares valuation fields, not their exposure-low projection; the reported joined check therefore leaves this disagreement intact.
   32| 
   33| **Consequence:** Following the canonical branch fails the supplied expected result; matching the expectation discards supported financial information. Retaining the high does not reconcile these authorities.
   34| 
   35| **Origin:** Unestablished introduction; current specification/corpus disagreement is explicit.
   36| 
   37| ### A-03 — P2: Lawful orphan evidence cannot reach an X result
   38| 
   39| **Location:** `spec/ck.json:2`, `CORRELATION C-7`, `S-zero-zero`, `V-PRESENCE-INTERFACE`, `X-BRANCH-005`, `X-BRANCH-016`, `INV-F9`.
   40| 
   41| **Counterexample:** A subscription-launched source contains only an Anthropic `response_model_refused` row, with no reservation, settlement or usage. C-7 produces an orphan Row. S-zero-zero supplies `not_applicable`; absent usage produces `not_reported`.
   42| 
   43| Keeping the subscription dimension hits excluded X-16. Reclassifying the contradicted lane as PiApi hits excluded X-5. Forcing valuation `not_applicable` instead reaches clean Codex, contrary to lane-contradiction policy.
   44| 
   45| **Consequence:** Explicit subscription-plus-reservation branches do not establish total applicability. This zero-known-component orphan requires an unresolved PiApi result, but reaching one requires an undeclared override of S.
   46| 
   47| **Origin:** New R7 exclusion/witness-interface interaction.
   48| 
   49| ### A-04 — P2: “Total projections” still do not describe the external representations
   50| 
   51| **Location:** `spec/ck.json:2`, `PROJECT-Exposure-pi_api`, `PROJECT-High-exact`, `PROJECT-Valuation-valued`; `spec/interface.json:2`; `tooling/src/decisions.rs:329–336,442–467`; `cases/U08-missing-observed-tier.json:2`.
   52| 
   53| **Failure conditions:**
   54| 
   55| - Exposure’s declared wire discriminator is `variant`; corpus exposure uses `pool`.
   56| - High declares `variant` plus nested `bound`; corpus high uses `kind`, `nano`, `basis`.
   57| - Valuation projects Bound-valued lows/highs and `splits`; corpus uses amount strings and `derivations`.
   58| 
   59| No declared transformation resolves these differences; projection validation requires identical internal field names.
   60| 
   61| Additionally, U08 still emits `TierUnestablished`, while the interface permits only the qualified Missing/Unrecognised codes.
   62| 
   63| **Consequence:** Internal constructor coverage is not total external projection coverage. Correct corpus values cannot be mapped by the supplied declarations alone.
   64| 
   65| **Origin:** Inherited interface-agreement failure, including the previously identified U08 code; the insufficient projection realization is new in R7.
   66| 
   67| ### A-05 — P2: S’s supposedly immutable witness projection remains independently editable
   68| 
   69| **Location:** `spec/ck.json:2`, `SETTLEMENT-WITNESS`, `S-unique-one-pass`, `S-unique-one-fail`; `tooling/src/lib.rs:534–551,576–586`.
   70| 
   71| **Executable failure condition:** Replace `S-unique-one-fail.outputs` with the output constructor from `S-unique-one-pass`. Keep its failing-check guard unchanged. Constructor fields and partition coverage remain valid; neither `validate` nor wire-interface checking verifies that this branch projects a rejected witness rather than `Valid`.
   72| 
   73| **Consequence:** The textual C-validity owner and S’s result table can disagree while declaration checks pass. This preserves the synchronization failure class at an expressly protected causal boundary—not merely an unimplemented raw reducer feature.
   74| 
   75| **Origin:** New R7 witness-projection realization.
   76| 
   77| ### A-06 — P2: Semantic changes still bypass affected consumer invalidation
   78| 
   79| **Location:** `spec/dependencies.json:2`; `spec/ck.json:2`, `USAGE-PRESENCE`, `VALUATION`, stage `V`; `tooling/src/lib.rs:160–233,787–822,1060–1093`.
   80| 
   81| **Executable failure condition:** Change `USAGE-PRESENCE`’s completeness policy and compare consumer digests or call `affected_consumers` with that node. `view-contract` and `consumer-R` are not reached: V’s stage edge points to C, not its valuation predicates, and those consumers lack another relevant dependency path.
   82| 
   83| Yet that predicate changes replacement, billing, predecessor exposure and financial provisionality.
   84| 
   85| **Consequence:** Whole-file generated-byte checking detects different bytes, but registered consumer acknowledgements need not become stale. Current null acknowledgements are not approvals; the invalidation mechanism remains insufficient.
   86| 
   87| **Origin:** Inherited dependency-completeness failure class; exact edge introduction unestablished.
   88| 
   89| ### A-07 — P2: Independent arithmetic silently ignores malformed model evidence
   90| 
   91| **Location:** `tooling/tests/independent_raw_axes.rs:106–120,151–162,245–249,895–905`; `spec/ck.json:2`, `VALUATION V-1`.
   92| 
   93| **Executable failure condition:** Take VC01’s request and replace `response_model` with numeric `7`. `identities` silently skips it, retaining the valid launch/request identity. `price_range` still returns `(15000000,15000000,true)`, and `accepts_usage_axis` accepts the unchanged eligible expectation.
   94| 
   95| V-1 instead makes a malformed model name an unpriceable identity.
   96| 
   97| **Consequence:** This is a false positive on the oracle’s declared identity/completeness axis, potentially supporting replacement without an established identity set. It is not an objection to approved fully priced multi-identity bounds, nor a demonstrated full-gate bypass.
   98| 
   99| **Origin:** Unestablished.
  100| 
  101| ### A-08 — P2: Unique-reservation applicability is reported from unique amounts
  102| 
  103| **Location:** `tooling/tests/independent_raw_axes.rs:342–359`; `spec/ck.json:2`, `CORRELATION C-1`, `SETTLEMENT-WITNESS`; `root/POLICY-AND-PLAN-APPROVAL.md:13`.
  104| 
  105| **Counterexample:** Two reservation rows share rid and amount 500M but differ in timestamp. They are distinct conflicting reservations. Their distinct-amount list nevertheless has length one.
  106| 
  107| The report consequently emits both `conflict=true` and `comparison_applicable=true`, because applicability uses `nums.len()==1`.
  108| 
  109| **Consequence:** An axis labelled established authorizes reservation-relative comparisons without the required exactly-one-distinct-reservation witness. Arithmetic uniqueness is not evidence uniqueness.
  110| 
  111| **Origin:** Unestablished.
  112| 
  113| ### A-09 — P2: The published JSON schema rejects the canonical specification
  114| 
  115| **Location:** `spec/ck.schema.json:2`, `properties.boundaries`, `$defs.Stage.properties.id`; `spec/ck.json:2`, `boundaries`; `tooling/src/lib.rs:125–131`.
  116| 
  117| **Executable failure condition:** Validate `spec/ck.json` with a Draft 2020-12 validator. Boundaries reuse `Stage`, whose identifier enum permits only E/C/S/V/F/X/K. Consequently `C-primary`, `C-validity`, `C-auxiliary` and `S-consume` fail schema validation.
  118| 
  119| The Rust loader compares schema bytes and deserializes into String-valued identifiers; it does not enforce that schema.
  120| 
  121| **Consequence:** Standard schema consumers and the qualification tool disagree about the canonical declaration’s validity.
  122| 
  123| **Origin:** New R7 boundary-schema defect.
  124| 
  125| ## Coverage, design implications and uncertainty
  126| 
  127| The delivered packet is complete, but its qualification evidence is deliberately bounded. Missing evaluations prevent independent checking of objective rollups and mock-gate residual outputs; omitted validator source prevents assessing its actual semantic checks.
  128| 
  129| The architecture does not yet prevent the ownership failure class. Result-bearing X branches constrain component selection **after classification**, but cannot repair changing correlation identities, unreachable input combinations, or independently maintained expected projections. A-01 demonstrates an I-1 failure beyond the eight previously identified examples.
  130| 
  131| Retain the financial distinctions. Revisit two boundaries: **C identity/merge accounting versus aggregate exposure**, and **owned declarations versus external/corpus qualification**. Stable provisional correlation identities or explicit merge accounting compete with the current Row-to-Reservation rekeying under max-and-sum arithmetic. Explicit external projections and independent boundary assertions compete with internal-constructor agreement plus disconnected expected outputs. Neither issue calls for a disguised reference reducer.
  132| 
  133| Status/terminal completeness, reservation-relative applicability, error usage, unknown-cache extrema and multi-identity bounds were examined. The findings distinguish malformed/unestablished identity from the permitted complete priced identity set. Supported arithmetic examples do not establish writer reachability, all combinations, full classifier correctness or live authority.
  134| 
  135| The raw audit’s pre-implementation freeze remains useful evidence for its stated axes only. Current tests and shared serialization mechanics do not establish complete semantic independence or full raw financial conformance.

    1| # Root disposition of R7 Module A

   27| ## 1. Source scope, claim identity and multiplicity
   28| 
   29| ### What the producer establishes
   30| 
   31| Pinned producer excerpt `/home/bline/cbm-work/p134-module-a/packet/sources/producer/pi_container.py.excerpts`, SHA256 `bfd226df9fc1fb8b5395a74490e3c1696273fab08e15d0e4da19b951320bb4b8`, preserves original source line numbers. `UsdBudget.__init__` resets `seq=0` at 970; successful reserve increments it under the caller's lock and creates ticket rid at 989–992; settle copies that ticket rid at 1018. Reservation writer rows carry it at 1187 and 1446, and settlement wrappers use the ticket at 1191–1197 and 1450–1455. Thus rid is a ticket reference within one budget/proxy incarnation; it is not a globally unique attempt identifier.
   32| 
   33| `accepted` resets at 1105, increments to n at 1346–1352, and decrements on release at 1358–1360. Consequently n is not a substitute stable ID, and n equality, amount equality or temporal proximity cannot establish identity. Subscription has its own serial at 882/889–891: namespace dollar ticket references separately from admission references. The logger appends to a supplied path at 1117–1119; these excerpts do **not** establish one incarnation per arbitrary log path. The existing C-11 one-source-per-turn rule is a model input contract, not proof of live realization.
   34| 
   35| ### The minimal identity structure
   36| 
   37| Separate three existing conflations:
   38| 
   39| ```
   40| SourceScope       = immutable logical stream identity in an admitted input world
   41| SourceRevision    = source bytes/hash and their observation provenance
   42| EvidenceIdentity  = original row identity plus its exact byte digest
   43| FinancialKey      = TicketClaim{SourceScope, dollar_rid}
   44|                   | AdmissionClaim{SourceScope, admission_rid}
   45|                   | UnattributedAtom{SourceScope/observation, EvidenceIdentity}
   46| ```
   47| 
   48| E currently calls SourceId a logical path plus file SHA256 (`ck.json`, EVIDENCE). A file-content digest is revision evidence; it cannot be the continuity identity for an append-extension comparison. Retain hashes on evidence and source revisions. The stable logical identity is bound separately to the session/turn/log relationship, with continuity provenance. Nothing joins across distinct admitted scopes. Aliases alone, matching hashes and matching paths do not prove independence or continuity; E's existing copied/shared/missing-log inventory remains relevant.
   49| 
   50| Within an admitted single reference scope, **all dollar reservation and settlement claims with usable rid select TicketClaim from the first observation, even without R**. Absence of R is a witness state, not a different financial identity. A reference group is not a claim that all its rows are genuine, or that its physical multiplicity is established. Keep every distinct evidence row, reservation cardinality, settlement cardinality, scope basis and any contradictory claims. Repeated identical evidence keeps existing repeat handling.
   51| 
   52| Distinct usable rid values in one admitted writer scope remain distinct financial units. Independently identified producer incarnations remain distinct scopes even if log path and rid match. An affirmative independently distinct attempt identity must never be absorbed into another group because its rid or amount matches. If explicit independent identities conflict with the supplied scope, admit their separate units or use the unestablished-scope path; preserve the contradiction. The absence of such evidence does not prove one physical attempt.
   53| 
   54| For same-reference conflicting rows whose independence is not established, keep the current max-of-claims arithmetic and AtLeast tag; do not sum them merely because timestamps or amounts differ. Record physical multiplicity as unestablished in the correlation witness, and keep acceptance blocked. AtLeast is an unresolved bound form, not permission to discard affirmative evidence of independent attempts. No finite billing ceiling follows from it.
   55| 
   56| ### Exact fallback and extension behavior
   57| 
   58| When there is no usable rid, its raw value remains malformed/absent and the row is an UnattributedAtom permanently; a later well-formed reservation cannot repair the old row's reference. When continuity/scope itself is unestablished, reservation rows and settlement rows likewise retain separate immutable monetary atoms: known R on its reservation atom, well-formed claimed charge and supported valuation on its settlement atom. Their possible co-reference stays an ambiguity relation, not a financial merge. Every known atom remains visible and additive; byte-identical repeats retain the existing dedup contract. This may overstate a bill, but it does not declare an Exact result or silently subtract an unmatched obligation.
   59| 
   60| Adding an R, an n match, amount match or later scope assertion never merges those fallback financial atoms or releases them. A scope upgrade must not retroactively erase their exposure. Scope assignment/provenance for already admitted evidence is immutable; qualifying a replacement input subject is an E/owner operation, not an exception to evidence-addition monotonicity. If an implementation cannot carry that boundary, stable-ref grouping is not qualified for that input. An eligible affirmative UsageBound may replace only the reservation unit it actually validates; it cannot cancel unrelated fallback charge atoms. Under unestablished scope no such linkage is established, so no replacement is eligible merely from matching rid.

It does not introduce a financial event-history accumulator.    76| ## 2. A result-bearing validity witness, then consumers
   77| 
   78| C-primary owns reference groups and distinct evidence identity. UniqueReservation means exactly one **distinct reservation evidence item**, not one unique amount. Two different rows of 500M are conflicting and cannot enable Order/equality/KeepAll comparisons.
